AbilitySlice [java.lang.Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) |---[ohos.app.AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759) |---|---ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice
    
    public class AbilitySlice
    extends [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)
    implements [ILifecycle](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ilifecycle-0000001054238677)  
  
---  
As the fundamental unit of the [Ability](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007) using the Page template, AbilitySlice provides an ability with a carrier for service logic and UI display. An ability can have multiple ability slices. You must override the [Ability#onStart(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to specify the default ability slice. This default route is specified by using [Ability#setMainRoute(String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) . You can inherit the [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class to implement your own ability slices and set their UI in the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method. The UI can be set by using [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) or [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-). Generally, in the entire lifecycle of an ability slice, the UI needs to be set only once. Example code:
    
    public class MyAbilitySlice extends AbilitySlice {
         protected void onStart(Intent intent) {
             super.onStart(intent);
             // setup UI content from a layout resource
             setUIContent(R.res.layout);
         }
     }

You can use the [present(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__present-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to present a new ability slice or use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start a new ability. An ability slice must always be hosted in an ability, and its lifecycle changes along with that of the ability. An ability slice also has its independent lifecycle changes, which occur during the switching of ability slices. **Note** : In most cases, you are responsible for creating and maintaining ability slice instances. However, the system creates instances in certain scenarios. For example, the system creates an ability slice instance during the first start of an ability. This requires that all [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) child classes have a default no-argument constructor. As defined in Java, the compiler provides a default no-argument constructor for the class only if you do not explicitly declare a parameterized constructor. Therefore, when overriding a [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) child class, you must explicitly declare a no-argument constructor if your implementation has a parameterized constructor. **Entry of an Ability Using the Page Template** You can specify multiple action entries and a default main entry for an ability. Each entry represents the capabilities and UI that the ability can provide. When an ability is started, the system matches the action field in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) against the entries defined. If a specified ability slice is found, the system starts it. If the action field is not specified in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) or the action field in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) does not match any entry defined, the default ability slice is displayed. The code below shows how to set the entry to an ability:
    
    public class MainAbility extends Ability {
         public void onStart(Intent intent) {
             super.onStart(intent);
             // set the main entry
             setMainRoute(MainSlice.class.getName());
    
             // add action for ability
             addActionRoute("action.pay", PaySlice.class.getName());
             addActionRoute("action.scan", ScanSlice.class.getName());
         }
     }
     

The preceding code defines only the entry route at runtime. To expose the ability's capabilities to other abilities, you must register the action field in the configuration file **config.json**. Example code:
    
    {
         "module":{
             ...
             "abilities":[
               {
                   ...
                   "description": "Main ability of hiworld",
                   "name": ".MainAbility",
                   "label": "main ability",
                   "icon": "main-ability.png",
                   "type": "page",
                   "visible": true,
                   "orientation": "unspecified",
                   "launch-mode": "standard",
                   "skills"[
                       {
                           "actions":[
                               "action.pay",
                               "action.scan"
                           ]
                       }
                   ]
                   ...
               }
             ]
             ...
         }
     }
     

After the supported actions are registered in the preceding way, other abilities can use your ability through the specified actions and directly open the corresponding ability slices. Example code:
    
    Intent intent = new Intent();
         Operation operation = new Intent.OperationBuilder()
                 .withAction("action.pay")
                 .withDeviceId("")
                 .withBundleName("xxx")
                 .withAbilityName("yyy")
                 .build();
         intent.setOperation(operation);
         startAbility(intent);
         
     

**AbilitySlice Lifecycle** An ability slice's lifecycle is bound to the ability that hosts it. An ability slice has the same lifecycle states and lifecycle callback methods as the host ability. Once the lifecycle state of an ability changes, its ability slices undergo the same change, and the same callback method is called. An ability slice can also have its independent lifecycle changes. This independent change occurs during the switching of the ability slices in the ability. However, such a switching does not affect the host ability's lifecycle. An ability slice has the following four lifecycle states:
                * **INITIAL:** The ability slice is loaded to the memory but is not running. It is the initial state of all ability slices.
                * **INACTIVE:** The ability slice has been loaded and executed but is not interactive. Generally, it is an intermediate state before the ability slice changes to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. The UI of an ability slice in this state may be visible but cannot receive input events.
                * **ACTIVE:** The ability slice is visible and interactive. It is considered that the ability slice has focus. 
                * **BACKGROUND** : The ability slice is invisible. If the host ability is also in the background, the ability and ability slices are destroyed in the case of insufficient memory.
Similar to an ability, an ability slice provides the following lifecycle callback methods. You can override them. 
    
    public class MainAbilitySlice extends AbilitySlice {
          protected void onStart(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onActive();
    
          protected void onInactive();
    
          protected void onForeground(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onBackground();
    
          protected void onStop();
      }
     
     

The figure below shows a complete lifecycle of an ability slice. An ability slice can be in only one state at any time. Not all states support direct transition. ![](https://communityfile-drcn.op.dbankcloud.cn/FileServer/getFile/cmtyPub/011/111/111/0000000000011111111.20231116085834.48664730397585020584549856716723:50001231000000:2800:A2EE249D91D1B90A05244043C428BDE6BCBA890FF1DB4637FE1026D016F92AC7.png?needInitFileName=true?needInitFileName=true) **Note** : The [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle. The meaning of each lifecycle callback method and some best practice suggestions is provided below.
                * **[onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-):** You must implement this method for UI initialization settings, for example, [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-). This method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle. The caller may carry some customized key-values by using Intent class, and obtain these parameters through the methods provided by intent. 
                * **[onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onActive--):** This method is called back when an ability slice returns to the **Active** state. After the callback, the UI is interactive. 
                * **[onInactive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onInactive--):** This method is called back when an ability slice is completely or partially covered. After the callback, the UI is not interactive. 
                * **[onForeground(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onForeground-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) ()}:** This method is called back when an ability slice returns to the foreground. The interaction logic of the application can be restored or initialized in the callback. If a new customized parameter needs to be specified when the ability slice returns to the foreground, you can transfer a new intent parameter. 
                * **[onBackground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onBackground--):** This method is called back when an ability slice has entered the background. In this state, you cannot perform background tasks for a long time, since the system may stop the running of a background program at any time. 
                * **[onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStop--):** This method is called back to destroy an ability slice. You can use this method to reclaim resources. 
**Switching Between Ability Slices** You can use the [present(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__present-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to present a new ability slice, and transfer customized parameters using Intent. Example code:
    
    Button button = new Button(this);
         button.setClickedListener(listener -> {
             AbilitySlice targetSlice = new MyAbilitySlice();
             Intent intent = new Intent();
             intent.setParam("value", 10);
             present(targetSlice, intent);
         });
     
     

**Note** : A maximum of 1024 ability slices in an ability can be presented simultaneously. If you attempt to present the 1025th ability slice, the system will forcibly crash the ability. To avoid this situation, you must improve the code if a large number of ability slices need to be presented simultaneously. **Since:** 1Nested Class Summary Modifier and Type | Class | Description

Field Detail [h2]PARAM_FORM_CUSTOMIZE_KEY public static final [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) PARAM_FORM_CUSTOMIZE_KEY Indicates the key used for passing custom data to the form provider when you call {@link #acquireForm(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice.FormCallback), which is represented as intent.setParam(PARAM_FORM_CUSTOMIZE_KEY, intentParams). You can put the custom data in an [IntentParams](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intentparams-0000001054439977) object and then set this object to the [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) used as an input parameter of {@link #acquireForm(Intent, FormCallback). Currently, the following data types are available for setting custom data. If an unavailable data type is used, [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) is thrown. 
                * boolean
                * byte
                * char
                * short
                * int
                * long
                * float
                * double
                * String
                * CharSequence
                * List
                * boolean[]
                * byte[]
                * char[]
                * short[]
                * int[]
                * long[]
                * float[]
                * double[]
                * String[]
                * CharSequence[]
**Since:** 5 **See Also:** [Constant Field the name of the module to which the form to be obtained belongs, which is represented as intent.setParam(PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY, "formEntry"). This constant is mandatory. **Since:** 5 **See Also:** [Constant Field Values](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/constant-values-0000001054359004#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054359004__ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice_PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY) Constructor Detail [h2]AbilitySlice public AbilitySlice​() Method Detail [h2]onStart protected void onStart​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Called back when an ability slice is started. The startup information is transferred using the intent parameter. You must override this method for each ability slice, and use the [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-) or [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) method to set the UI. This method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle of an ability slice. Therefore, you are advised to use this method to implement initialization, for example, initializing a timer or defining a global object. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when the ability slice is started.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onActive protected void onActive​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **ACTIVE** state. The UI of an ability slice in the **ACTIVE** state has gained focus and is interactive.  **Since:** 1 [h2]onInactive protected void onInactive​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **INACTIVE** state. **INACTIVE** is an intermediate state and may immediately change to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. The UI of an ability slice in this state may be visible but is not interactive. You are advised not to use this method to trigger complex service logic. **Since:** 1 [h2]onForeground protected void onForeground​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Called back when the state of an ability slice changes from **BACKGROUND** to **INACTIVE**. The ability slice returns to the foreground. You can use this method to implement reinitialization or adjust the UI by using the intent parameter. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the information carried when the ability slice returns to the **INACTIVE** state.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onBackground protected void onBackground​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **BACKGROUND** state. The UI of an ability slice in the **BACKGROUND** state is invisible and not interactive. You are advised to use this method to stop or release resources. **Since:** 1 [h2]onStop protected void onStop​() Called back before an ability slice is destroyed. This method is called back after an ability slice calls the [terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method. You can use this method to reclaim resources. **Since:** 1 [h2]onAbilityResult protected void onAbilityResult​(int requestCode, int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData) Called when [startAbilityForResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start an ability and the result is returned. This method provides the same function as the [Ability#onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method of the ability. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is started. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is started. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [startAbilityForResult(Intent, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) [h2]onResult protected void onResult​(int requestCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultIntent) Called back when the result of the [presentForResult(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) method is returned. When [presentForResult(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start a new ability slice, the execution result is returned when the ability slice is destroyed. You can override this method to process the result. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability slice is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by ability slices. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultIntent  |  Indicates the data returned after the started ability slice is destroyed. This parameter can be null.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [presentForResult(AbilitySlice, Intent, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-), [setResult(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]onOrientationChanged protected void onOrientationChanged​([AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810) displayOrientation) Called back when the display orientation of a screen changes. You can override this method to implement your logic when the display orientation changes. By default, no operation is performed when the display orientation changes. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
displayOrientation  |  Indicates the new display orientation. The following values are supported: [DisplayOrientation#LANDSCAPE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__LANDSCAPE), [DisplayOrientation#PORTRAIT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__PORTRAIT), [DisplayOrientation#FOLLOWRECENT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__FOLLOWRECENT), and [DisplayOrientation#UNSPECIFIED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__UNSPECIFIED).  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onBackPressed protected void onBackPressed​() Called back when the Back key is pressed. The default implementation destroys the ability slice. You can override this method. **Since:** 1 [h2]onKeyDown public boolean onKeyDown​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed. When any component in the AbilitySlice gains focus, the key-down event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-down event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key pressed.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-down event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyUp(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onKeyUp-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]onKeyUp public boolean onKeyUp​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is released. When any component in the AbilitySlice gains focus, the key-up event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-up event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key released.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-up event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyDown(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onKeyDown-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]postTask public void postTask​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread. The task posted via this method will be executed in a new thread, which allows you to perform certain time-consuming operations. To use this method, you must also override the [Ability#supportHighPerformanceUI()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__supportHighPerformanceUI--) method. Additionally, the usage of this method must comply with the following constraints: 
                1. This method can only be used to initialize the component tree in parallel mode.
                2. The task can only be processed during system cold start.
                3. If the parallel loading mechanism is used, the component tree-related operations to be performed in [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onActive--) and [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStop--) of the ability slice must also be added to the parallel thread.
                4. You must run [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) to unlock the UI thread and wait until the component tree is ready.
                5. Other time-consuming operations, such as I/O and network processing, cannot be added to the parallel task queue.
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the Runnable task to post.  
delayTime  |  Indicates the number of milliseconds after which the task will be executed.  
**Since:** 4 [h2]onHotkeyTriggered public boolean onHotkeyTriggered​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a hotkey is pressed. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the key code.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.

---|---

keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the key event is handled in this callback; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 5 [h2]toString public [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) toString​() Provide impl and corresponding slice info for easy human reading traces. **Overrides:** [toString](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__toString--) in class [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) **Returns:** Info in String format. **Since:** 1 [h2]getWindow public [Window](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/window-0000001054358748) getWindow​() Obtains the window corresponding to the current ability. Layout parameters must be set for windows of all abilities. These parameters are provided for the parent window to specify how to lay out the windows of the current ability. This method provides the same function as the [Ability#getLayoutParams()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__getLayoutParams--) method. **Returns:** Returns window layout parameters of the ability; returns null if no layout parameter is set. **Since:** 1 [h2]setLayoutParams public void setLayoutParams​([WindowManager.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/windowmanager_layoutconfig-0000001054120018) params) Sets window layout parameters of an ability. This method provides the same function as the [Ability#setLayoutParams(LayoutConfig)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setLayoutParams-ohos_agp_window_service_WindowManager_LayoutConfig-) method. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description

**Since:** 3 [h2]getContinuationRegisterManager public [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) getContinuationRegisterManager​() Obtains an [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object. You can use the [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object to interact with the hop task management service, including registering and unregistering the ability to migrate, updating the device connection state, and showing the list of devices that can be selected for ability migration. **Returns:** Returns the [IContinuationRegisterManager](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/icontinuationregistermanager-0000001138987467) object. **Since:** 5 [h2]setUIContent public final void setUIContent​(int layoutRes) Inflates layout resources by using the layout resource ID. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description

---|---

---|---  
resId  |  Indicates the resource ID of the component.  
**Returns:** Returns the component corresponding to the ID if a record is found; returns null otherwise. **Since:** 1 [h2]present public final void present​([AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) targetSlice, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Presents another ability slice, which can be an ability slice that is not started or an existing ability slice in the host ability. You can use an [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) object to pass required information. This method can be called only in the following cases:
                * The host ability is in the **ACTIVE** state.
                * The target ability slice is not started or destroyed.
                * The number of ability slices in the host ability does not exceed 1024.
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
targetSlice  |  Indicates the target ability slice, which cannot be null.  
intent  |  Indicates information carried during the switching, which cannot be null.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if targetSlice is invalid.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [presentForResult(AbilitySlice, Intent, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) [h2]presentForResult public final void presentForResult​([AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) targetSlice, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, int requestCode) Presents another ability slice and returns the result set by the target ability slice by calling [setResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-). You can use an [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) object to pass required information. This method can be called only in the following cases:
                * The host ability of the current ability slice is in the **ACTIVE** state.
                * The target ability slice is not started or destroyed.
                * There are 1024 or less ability slices in the host ability.
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
targetSlice  |  Indicates the target ability slice. This parameter cannot be null.  
intent  |  Indicates information carried during the switching, which cannot be null.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the customized request code, which cannot be a negative number.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if targetSlice or requestCode is invalid.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [setResult(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [onResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]setResult public final void setResult​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData) Sets the data to be returned when an ability slice is destroyed. When the ability slice calls the [terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method, the result data set by the current method is transferred to the caller. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability slice is destroyed. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [presentForResult(AbilitySlice, Intent, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-), [onResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]terminate public final void terminate​() Destroys the current ability slice. When the current ability slice calls this method to destroy itself, the result data set by [setResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is returned to the caller. If the slice to be destroyed is the only slice of its host ability, the ability will also be destroyed. Otherwise, the ability will not be affected. **Since:** 1 [h2]startAbility public void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Starts a new ability. An ability slice can also use this method to start a new ability. Similar to the function of the [Ability#startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method, the system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]startAbility public void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting) Starts a new ability with specific start settings. An ability slice can also use this method to start a new ability. Similar to the function of the [Ability#startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method, the system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---

---|---

When the ability is destroyed, [onAbilityResult(int,int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is called and the returned requestCode is transferred to the current method. ---|---

---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.

    public class AbilitySlice
    extends [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)
    implements [ILifecycle](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ilifecycle-0000001054238677)  
  
---  
As the fundamental unit of the [Ability](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007) using the Page template, AbilitySlice provides an ability with a carrier for service logic and UI display. An ability can have multiple ability slices. You must override the [Ability#onStart(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to specify the default ability slice. This default route is specified by using [Ability#setMainRoute(String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) . You can inherit the [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class to implement your own ability slices and set their UI in the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method. The UI can be set by using [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) or [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-). Generally, in the entire lifecycle of an ability slice, the UI needs to be set only once. Example code:
    
    public class MyAbilitySlice extends AbilitySlice {
         protected void onStart(Intent intent) {
             super.onStart(intent);
             // setup UI content from a layout resource
             setUIContent(R.res.layout);
         }
     }

You can use the [present(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__present-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to present a new ability slice or use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start a new ability. An ability slice must always be hosted in an ability, and its lifecycle changes along with that of the ability. An ability slice also has its independent lifecycle changes, which occur during the switching of ability slices. **Note** : In most cases, you are responsible for creating and maintaining ability slice instances. However, the system creates instances in certain scenarios. For example, the system creates an ability slice instance during the first start of an ability. This requires that all [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) child classes have a default no-argument constructor. As defined in Java, the compiler provides a default no-argument constructor for the class only if you do not explicitly declare a parameterized constructor. Therefore, when overriding a [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) child class, you must explicitly declare a no-argument constructor if your implementation has a parameterized constructor. **Entry of an Ability Using the Page Template** You can specify multiple action entries and a default main entry for an ability. Each entry represents the capabilities and UI that the ability can provide. When an ability is started, the system matches the action field in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) against the entries defined. If a specified ability slice is found, the system starts it. If the action field is not specified in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) or the action field in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) does not match any entry defined, the default ability slice is displayed. The code below shows how to set the entry to an ability:
    
    public class MainAbility extends Ability {
         public void onStart(Intent intent) {
             super.onStart(intent);
             // set the main entry
             setMainRoute(MainSlice.class.getName());
    
             // add action for ability
             addActionRoute("action.pay", PaySlice.class.getName());
             addActionRoute("action.scan", ScanSlice.class.getName());
         }
     }
     

The preceding code defines only the entry route at runtime. To expose the ability's capabilities to other abilities, you must register the action field in the configuration file **config.json**. Example code:
    
    {
         "module":{
             ...
             "abilities":[
               {
                   ...
                   "description": "Main ability of hiworld",
                   "name": ".MainAbility",
                   "label": "main ability",
                   "icon": "main-ability.png",
                   "type": "page",
                   "visible": true,
                   "orientation": "unspecified",
                   "launch-mode": "standard",
                   "skills"[
                       {
                           "actions":[
                               "action.pay",
                               "action.scan"
                           ]
                       }
                   ]
                   ...
               }
             ]
             ...
         }
     }
     

After the supported actions are registered in the preceding way, other abilities can use your ability through the specified actions and directly open the corresponding ability slices. Example code:
    
    Intent intent = new Intent();
         Operation operation = new Intent.OperationBuilder()
                 .withAction("action.pay")
                 .withDeviceId("")
                 .withBundleName("xxx")
                 .withAbilityName("yyy")
                 .build();
         intent.setOperation(operation);
         startAbility(intent);
         
     

**AbilitySlice Lifecycle** An ability slice's lifecycle is bound to the ability that hosts it. An ability slice has the same lifecycle states and lifecycle callback methods as the host ability. Once the lifecycle state of an ability changes, its ability slices undergo the same change, and the same callback method is called. An ability slice can also have its independent lifecycle changes. This independent change occurs during the switching of the ability slices in the ability. However, such a switching does not affect the host ability's lifecycle. An ability slice has the following four lifecycle states:
                * **INITIAL:** The ability slice is loaded to the memory but is not running. It is the initial state of all ability slices.
                * **INACTIVE:** The ability slice has been loaded and executed but is not interactive. Generally, it is an intermediate state before the ability slice changes to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. The UI of an ability slice in this state may be visible but cannot receive input events.
                * **ACTIVE:** The ability slice is visible and interactive. It is considered that the ability slice has focus. 
                * **BACKGROUND** : The ability slice is invisible. If the host ability is also in the background, the ability and ability slices are destroyed in the case of insufficient memory.
Similar to an ability, an ability slice provides the following lifecycle callback methods. You can override them. 
    
    public class MainAbilitySlice extends AbilitySlice {
          protected void onStart(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onActive();
    
          protected void onInactive();
    
          protected void onForeground(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onBackground();
    
          protected void onStop();
      }
     
     

The figure below shows a complete lifecycle of an ability slice. An ability slice can be in only one state at any time. Not all states support direct transition. ![](https://communityfile-drcn.op.dbankcloud.cn/FileServer/getFile/cmtyPub/011/111/111/0000000000011111111.20231116085834.48664730397585020584549856716723:50001231000000:2800:A2EE249D91D1B90A05244043C428BDE6BCBA890FF1DB4637FE1026D016F92AC7.png?needInitFileName=true?needInitFileName=true) **Note** : The [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle. The meaning of each lifecycle callback method and some best practice suggestions is provided below.
                * **[onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-):** You must implement this method for UI initialization settings, for example, [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-). This method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle. The caller may carry some customized key-values by using Intent class, and obtain these parameters through the methods provided by intent. 
                * **[onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onActive--):** This method is called back when an ability slice returns to the **Active** state. After the callback, the UI is interactive. 
                * **[onInactive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onInactive--):** This method is called back when an ability slice is completely or partially covered. After the callback, the UI is not interactive. 
                * **[onForeground(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onForeground-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) ()}:** This method is called back when an ability slice returns to the foreground. The interaction logic of the application can be restored or initialized in the callback. If a new customized parameter needs to be specified when the ability slice returns to the foreground, you can transfer a new intent parameter. 
                * **[onBackground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onBackground--):** This method is called back when an ability slice has entered the background. In this state, you cannot perform background tasks for a long time, since the system may stop the running of a background program at any time. 
                * **[onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStop--):** This method is called back to destroy an ability slice. You can use this method to reclaim resources. 
**Switching Between Ability Slices** You can use the [present(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__present-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to present a new ability slice, and transfer customized parameters using Intent. Example code:
    
    Button button = new Button(this);
         button.setClickedListener(listener -> {
             AbilitySlice targetSlice = new MyAbilitySlice();
             Intent intent = new Intent();
             intent.setParam("value", 10);
             present(targetSlice, intent);
         });
     
     

**Note** : A maximum of 1024 ability slices in an ability can be presented simultaneously. If you attempt to present the 1025th ability slice, the system will forcibly crash the ability. To avoid this situation, you must improve the code if a large number of ability slices need to be presented simultaneously. **Since:** 1Nested Class Summary Modifier and Type | Class | Description

---  
As the fundamental unit of the [Ability](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007) using the Page template, AbilitySlice provides an ability with a carrier for service logic and UI display. An ability can have multiple ability slices. You must override the [Ability#onStart(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to specify the default ability slice. This default route is specified by using [Ability#setMainRoute(String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__setMainRoute-java_lang_String-) . You can inherit the [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) class to implement your own ability slices and set their UI in the [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method. The UI can be set by using [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) or [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-). Generally, in the entire lifecycle of an ability slice, the UI needs to be set only once. Example code:
    
    public class MyAbilitySlice extends AbilitySlice {
         protected void onStart(Intent intent) {
             super.onStart(intent);
             // setup UI content from a layout resource
             setUIContent(R.res.layout);
         }
     }

You can use the [present(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__present-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to present a new ability slice or use the [startAbility(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to start a new ability. An ability slice must always be hosted in an ability, and its lifecycle changes along with that of the ability. An ability slice also has its independent lifecycle changes, which occur during the switching of ability slices. **Note** : In most cases, you are responsible for creating and maintaining ability slice instances. However, the system creates instances in certain scenarios. For example, the system creates an ability slice instance during the first start of an ability. This requires that all [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) child classes have a default no-argument constructor. As defined in Java, the compiler provides a default no-argument constructor for the class only if you do not explicitly declare a parameterized constructor. Therefore, when overriding a [AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) child class, you must explicitly declare a no-argument constructor if your implementation has a parameterized constructor. **Entry of an Ability Using the Page Template** You can specify multiple action entries and a default main entry for an ability. Each entry represents the capabilities and UI that the ability can provide. When an ability is started, the system matches the action field in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) against the entries defined. If a specified ability slice is found, the system starts it. If the action field is not specified in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) or the action field in the transferred [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) does not match any entry defined, the default ability slice is displayed. The code below shows how to set the entry to an ability:
    
    public class MainAbility extends Ability {
         public void onStart(Intent intent) {
             super.onStart(intent);
             // set the main entry
             setMainRoute(MainSlice.class.getName());
    
             // add action for ability
             addActionRoute("action.pay", PaySlice.class.getName());
             addActionRoute("action.scan", ScanSlice.class.getName());
         }
     }
     

The preceding code defines only the entry route at runtime. To expose the ability's capabilities to other abilities, you must register the action field in the configuration file **config.json**. Example code:
    
    {
         "module":{
             ...
             "abilities":[
               {
                   ...
                   "description": "Main ability of hiworld",
                   "name": ".MainAbility",
                   "label": "main ability",
                   "icon": "main-ability.png",
                   "type": "page",
                   "visible": true,
                   "orientation": "unspecified",
                   "launch-mode": "standard",
                   "skills"[
                       {
                           "actions":[
                               "action.pay",
                               "action.scan"
                           ]
                       }
                   ]
                   ...
               }
             ]
             ...
         }
     }
     

After the supported actions are registered in the preceding way, other abilities can use your ability through the specified actions and directly open the corresponding ability slices. Example code:
    
    Intent intent = new Intent();
         Operation operation = new Intent.OperationBuilder()
                 .withAction("action.pay")
                 .withDeviceId("")
                 .withBundleName("xxx")
                 .withAbilityName("yyy")
                 .build();
         intent.setOperation(operation);
         startAbility(intent);
         
     

**AbilitySlice Lifecycle** An ability slice's lifecycle is bound to the ability that hosts it. An ability slice has the same lifecycle states and lifecycle callback methods as the host ability. Once the lifecycle state of an ability changes, its ability slices undergo the same change, and the same callback method is called. An ability slice can also have its independent lifecycle changes. This independent change occurs during the switching of the ability slices in the ability. However, such a switching does not affect the host ability's lifecycle. An ability slice has the following four lifecycle states:
                * **INITIAL:** The ability slice is loaded to the memory but is not running. It is the initial state of all ability slices.
                * **INACTIVE:** The ability slice has been loaded and executed but is not interactive. Generally, it is an intermediate state before the ability slice changes to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. The UI of an ability slice in this state may be visible but cannot receive input events.
                * **ACTIVE:** The ability slice is visible and interactive. It is considered that the ability slice has focus. 
                * **BACKGROUND** : The ability slice is invisible. If the host ability is also in the background, the ability and ability slices are destroyed in the case of insufficient memory.
Similar to an ability, an ability slice provides the following lifecycle callback methods. You can override them. 
    
    public class MainAbilitySlice extends AbilitySlice {
          protected void onStart(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onActive();
    
          protected void onInactive();
    
          protected void onForeground(Intent intent);
    
          protected void onBackground();
    
          protected void onStop();
      }
     
     

The figure below shows a complete lifecycle of an ability slice. An ability slice can be in only one state at any time. Not all states support direct transition. ![](https://communityfile-drcn.op.dbankcloud.cn/FileServer/getFile/cmtyPub/011/111/111/0000000000011111111.20231116085834.48664730397585020584549856716723:50001231000000:2800:A2EE249D91D1B90A05244043C428BDE6BCBA890FF1DB4637FE1026D016F92AC7.png?needInitFileName=true?needInitFileName=true) **Note** : The [onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle. The meaning of each lifecycle callback method and some best practice suggestions is provided below.
                * **[onStart(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStart-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-):** You must implement this method for UI initialization settings, for example, [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-). This method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle. The caller may carry some customized key-values by using Intent class, and obtain these parameters through the methods provided by intent. 
                * **[onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onActive--):** This method is called back when an ability slice returns to the **Active** state. After the callback, the UI is interactive. 
                * **[onInactive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onInactive--):** This method is called back when an ability slice is completely or partially covered. After the callback, the UI is not interactive. 
                * **[onForeground(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onForeground-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) ()}:** This method is called back when an ability slice returns to the foreground. The interaction logic of the application can be restored or initialized in the callback. If a new customized parameter needs to be specified when the ability slice returns to the foreground, you can transfer a new intent parameter. 
                * **[onBackground()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onBackground--):** This method is called back when an ability slice has entered the background. In this state, you cannot perform background tasks for a long time, since the system may stop the running of a background program at any time. 
                * **[onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStop--):** This method is called back to destroy an ability slice. You can use this method to reclaim resources. 
**Switching Between Ability Slices** You can use the [present(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__present-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method to present a new ability slice, and transfer customized parameters using Intent. Example code:
    
    Button button = new Button(this);
         button.setClickedListener(listener -> {
             AbilitySlice targetSlice = new MyAbilitySlice();
             Intent intent = new Intent();
             intent.setParam("value", 10);
             present(targetSlice, intent);
         });

**Since:** 3 [h2]continueAbilityReversibly public void continueAbilityReversibly​([String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) deviceId) throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Migrates the host ability to another device on the same distributed network in a reversible way that allows the ability to be migrated back to the local device through [reverseContinueAbility()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__reverseContinueAbility--). This method provides the same function as the [Ability#continueAbilityReversibly(String)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__continueAbilityReversibly-java_lang_String-) method. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
deviceId  |  Indicates the ID of the target device where the host ability will be migrated to. If this parameter is null, this method has the same effect as [continueAbilityReversibly()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__continueAbilityReversibly--).  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452) |  Throws this exception if the host ability is not initialized or is in a state where this operation is not allowed, for example, it is being migrated.  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if the host ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
**Since:** 3 [h2]reverseContinueAbility public boolean reverseContinueAbility​() throws [IllegalStateException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalstateexception-0000001054678452), [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Migrates the host ability from another device on the same distributed network back to the local device. Obtains the migration state of the host ability. This method provides the same function as the [Ability#getContinuationState()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__getContinuationState--) method. **Returns:** Returns the migration state. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if the host ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
**Since:** 3 [h2]getOriginalDeviceId public final [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) getOriginalDeviceId​() throws [UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) Obtains the ID of the source device from which the host ability is migrated. This method provides the same function as the [Ability#getOriginalDeviceId()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__getOriginalDeviceId--) method. **Returns:** Returns the source device ID. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[UnsupportedOperationException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/unsupportedoperationexception-0000001054238453) |  Throws this exception if the host ability does not implement the IAbilityContinuation interface.  
**Since:** 3 [h2]setDisplayOrientation public void setDisplayOrientation​([AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810) requestedOrientation) Sets the display orientation of the host ability. After you change the display orientation using this method, the system calls back the [onOrientationChanged(ohos.bundle.AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onOrientationChanged-ohos_bundle_AbilityInfo_DisplayOrientation-) method. If requestedOrientation is set to [DisplayOrientation#UNSPECIFIED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__UNSPECIFIED), the display orientation of the ability depends on the system setting.  If an ability is in the foreground, the setting takes effect immediately. Otherwise, the setting takes effect when the ability returns to the foreground. **Specified by:** [setDisplayOrientation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__setDisplayOrientation-ohos_bundle_AbilityInfo_DisplayOrientation-) in interface [Context](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065) **Overrides:** [setDisplayOrientation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054358759__setDisplayOrientation-ohos_bundle_AbilityInfo_DisplayOrientation-) in class [AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestedOrientation  |  Indicates the display orientation, which can be UNSPECIFIED, LANDSCAPE, PORTRAIT, or FOLLOWRECENT.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if requestedOrientation is invalid. | Methods inherited from class ohos.app.[AbilityContext](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitycontext-0000001054358759)  
---

Field Detail [h2]PARAM_FORM_CUSTOMIZE_KEY public static final [String](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/string-0000001054518460) PARAM_FORM_CUSTOMIZE_KEY Indicates the key used for passing custom data to the form provider when you call {@link #acquireForm(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice.FormCallback), which is represented as intent.setParam(PARAM_FORM_CUSTOMIZE_KEY, intentParams). You can put the custom data in an [IntentParams](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intentparams-0000001054439977) object and then set this object to the [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) used as an input parameter of {@link #acquireForm(Intent, FormCallback). Currently, the following data types are available for setting custom data. If an unavailable data type is used, [IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) is thrown. 
                * boolean
                * byte
                * char
                * short
                * int
                * long
                * float
                * double
                * String
                * CharSequence
                * List
                * boolean[]
                * byte[]
                * char[]
                * short[]
                * int[]
                * long[]
                * float[]
                * double[]
                * String[]
                * CharSequence[]
**Since:** 5 **See Also:** [Constant Field
