**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [Context.setDisplayOrientation(AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/context-0000001054440065#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054440065__setDisplayOrientation-ohos_bundle_AbilityInfo_DisplayOrientation-) [h2]acquireAbilityFormAsync public boolean acquireAbilityFormAsync​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilityForm.OnAcquiredCallback](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform_onacquiredcallback-0000001054199950) acquiredCallback) Requests the [AbilityForm](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform-0000001054199952) instance provided by the target ability. This method is executed asynchronously. After the [AbilityForm](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform-0000001054199952) instance is obtained, the system calls back the [OnAcquiredCallback](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform_onacquiredcallback-0000001054199950) method. If the target ability does not use the Page template or does not provide the AbilityForm capability, the caller is not notified. To use this method, your application must have the ohos.permission.REQUIRE_FORM permission. This permission is available only to system applications. If the caller does not have the permission, false is returned. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the target ability.  
acquiredCallback  |  Indicates the notification callback after the [AbilityForm](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityform-0000001054199952) instance is asynchronously obtained.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the request is sent successfully; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if intent or acquiredCallback is invalid.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]acquireForm public boolean acquireForm​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilitySlice.FormCallback](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice_formcallback-0000001071073303) callback) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Obtains a specified form that matches the application bundle name, module name, form name, form grid style, and other related information specified in the passed Intent. This method is asynchronous. After the [Form](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/form-0000001071233243) instance is obtained, [AbilitySlice.FormCallback.onAcquired(int, Form)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice_formcallback-0000001071073303#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001071073303__onAcquired-int-ohos_aafwk_ability_Form-) will be invoked to notify the application.  **Permissions:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM); [SystemPermission.GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the detailed information about the form to be obtained, including the bundle name, module name, ability name, form name, form grid styles, form width and height, and form ID.  
callback  |  Indicates the callback to be invoked whenever the [Form](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/form-0000001071233243) instance is obtained.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the request is successfully initiated; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The input parameter is null.
                * The Intent does not bear the necessary bundleName, abilityName, and moduleName.
                * An error occurred when sending the request to the Form Manager Service.
                * No form can be found based on the passed parameters.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) or [SystemPermission.GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED) permission.
                * The form identified by the ID has been obtained by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
[h2]onStart protected void onStart​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Called back when an ability slice is started. The startup information is transferred using the intent parameter. You must override this method for each ability slice, and use the [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-) or [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) method to set the UI. This method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle of an ability slice. Therefore, you are advised to use this method to implement initialization, for example, initializing a timer or defining a global object. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when the ability slice is started.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onActive protected void onActive​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **ACTIVE** state. The UI of an ability slice in the **ACTIVE** state has gained focus and is interactive.  **Since:** 1 [h2]onInactive protected void onInactive​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **INACTIVE** state. **INACTIVE** is an intermediate state and may immediately change to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. The UI of an ability slice in this state may be visible but is not interactive. You are advised not to use this method to trigger complex service logic. **Since:** 1 [h2]onForeground protected void onForeground​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Called back when the state of an ability slice changes from **BACKGROUND** to **INACTIVE**. The ability slice returns to the foreground. You can use this method to implement reinitialization or adjust the UI by using the intent parameter. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the information carried when the ability slice returns to the **INACTIVE** state.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onBackground protected void onBackground​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **BACKGROUND** state. The UI of an ability slice in the **BACKGROUND** state is invisible and not interactive. You are advised to use this method to stop or release resources. **Since:** 1 [h2]onStop protected void onStop​() Called back before an ability slice is destroyed. This method is called back after an ability slice calls the [terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method. You can use this method to reclaim resources. **Since:** 1 [h2]onAbilityResult protected void onAbilityResult​(int requestCode, int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData) Called when [startAbilityForResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start an ability and the result is returned. This method provides the same function as the [Ability#onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method of the ability. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is started. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is started. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [startAbilityForResult(Intent, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) [h2]onResult protected void onResult​(int requestCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultIntent) Called back when the result of the [presentForResult(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) method is returned. When [presentForResult(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start a new ability slice, the execution result is returned when the ability slice is destroyed. You can override this method to process the result. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
[clone](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__clone--), [equals](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__equals-java_lang_Object-), [finalize](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__finalize--), [getClass](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__getClass--), [hashCode](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__hashCode--), [notify](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__notify--), [notifyAll](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__notifyAll--), [wait](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__wait--), [wait](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__wait-long-), [wait](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678446__wait-long-int-)

The following values are supported: [DisplayOrientation#LANDSCAPE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__LANDSCAPE), [DisplayOrientation#PORTRAIT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__PORTRAIT), [DisplayOrientation#FOLLOWRECENT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__FOLLOWRECENT), and [DisplayOrientation#UNSPECIFIED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__UNSPECIFIED).  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onBackPressed protected void onBackPressed​() Called back when the Back key is pressed. The default implementation destroys the ability slice. You can override this method. **Since:** 1 [h2]onKeyDown public boolean onKeyDown​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed. When any component in the AbilitySlice gains focus, the key-down event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-down event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key pressed.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-down event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyUp(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onKeyUp-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]onKeyUp public boolean onKeyUp​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is released. When any component in the AbilitySlice gains focus, the key-up event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-up event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key released.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-up event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyDown(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onKeyDown-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]postTask public void postTask​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread. The task posted via this method will be executed in a new thread, which allows you to perform certain time-consuming operations. To use this method, you must also override the [Ability#supportHighPerformanceUI()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__supportHighPerformanceUI--) method. Additionally, the usage of this method must comply with the following constraints: 
                1. This method can only be used to initialize the component tree in parallel mode.
                2. The task can only be processed during system cold start.
                3. If the parallel loading mechanism is used, the component tree-related operations to be performed in [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onActive--) and [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStop--) of the ability slice must also be added to the parallel thread.
                4. You must run [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) to unlock the UI thread and wait until the component tree is ready.
                5. Other time-consuming operations, such as I/O and network processing, cannot be added to the parallel task queue.
**Parameters:** [Constant Field Values](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/constant-values-0000001054359004#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054359004__ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice_PARAM_FORM_ID_KEY) This constant is mandatory. **Since:** 5 **See Also:** [Constant Field Values](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/constant-values-0000001054359004#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054359004__ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice_PARAM_MODULE_NAME_KEY) Constructor Detail [h2]AbilitySlice public AbilitySlice​() Method Detail [h2]onStart protected void onStart​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Called back when an ability slice is started. The startup information is transferred using the intent parameter. You must override this method for each ability slice, and use the [setUIContent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-int-) or [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) method to set the UI. This method can be called only once in the entire lifecycle of an ability slice. Therefore, you are advised to use this method to implement initialization, for example, initializing a timer or defining a global object. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates information carried when the ability slice is started.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onActive protected void onActive​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **ACTIVE** state. The UI of an ability slice in the **ACTIVE** state has gained focus and is interactive.  **Since:** 1 [h2]onInactive protected void onInactive​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **INACTIVE** state. **INACTIVE** is an intermediate state and may immediately change to **ACTIVE** or **BACKGROUND**. The UI of an ability slice in this state may be visible but is not interactive. You are advised not to use this method to trigger complex service logic. **Since:** **Since:** 5 [h2]deleteForm [@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public boolean deleteForm​(int formId) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [deleteForm(long)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__deleteForm-long-). Deletes an obtained form by its ID. After this method is called, the form won't be available for use by the application and the Form Manager Service no longer keeps the cache information about the form. **Permission:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the form ID, which can be obtained via [Form.getId()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/form-0000001071233243#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001071233243__getId--).  
**Returns:** Returns true if the form is successfully deleted; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be deleted due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formId is invalid. Its value must be larger than 0.
                * The specified form has not been added by the application.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be deleted by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.

**Returns:** Returns true if the form is successfully released; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be released due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formId is invalid. Its value must be larger than 0.
                * The specified form has not been added by the application.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be released by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.

**Since:** 5 [h2]castTempForm public boolean castTempForm​(long formId) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Converts a specified temporary form that has been obtained by the application into a normal form. After this method is successfully called, the temporary form will be converted to a normal form, the data in the cache will be updated accordingly, and the form data such as the form ID will be made persistent.  **Permissions:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the temporary form to convert, which is obtained via [Form.getId()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/form-0000001071233243#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001071233243__getId--).  
**Returns:** Returns true if the conversion is successful; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form fails to be converted due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formId is invalid. Its value must be larger than 0.
                * The specified form ID is not the ID of a temporary form. 
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The number of normal forms requested by the current form client in the cache has reached the upper limit (256). 
                * The number of Form instances in the cache of the Form Manager Service has reached the upper limit (512). 
                * The form is being restored.   
**Since:** 5 [h2]notifyVisibleForms public void notifyVisibleForms​([List](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/list-0000001054119842)<[Long](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/long-0000001054439731)> formIds) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Sends a notification to the form framework to make the specified forms visible. After this method is successfully called, [Ability.onEventNotify(Map<Long, int>)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onEventNotify-java_util_Map-) will be called to notify the form provider of the form visibility change event. **Permissions:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formIds  |  Indicates the IDs of the forms to be made visible.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the forms fail to be made visible due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formIds parameter is invalid. Each form ID in it must be larger than 0.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be changed by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.   
**Since:** 5 [h2]notifyInvisibleForms public void notifyInvisibleForms​([List](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/list-0000001054119842)<[Long](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/long-0000001054439731)> formIds) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Sends a notification to the form framework to make the specified forms invisible. After this method is successfully called, [Ability.onEventNotify(Map<Long, int>)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onEventNotify-java_util_Map-) will be called to notify the form provider of the form visibility change event. **Permissions:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formIds  |  Indicates the IDs of the forms to be made invisible.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the forms fail to be made invisible due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formIds parameter is invalid. Each form ID in it must be larger than 0.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be changed by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.   
**Since:** 5 [h2]checkAndDeleteInvalidForms public int checkAndDeleteInvalidForms​([List](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/list-0000001054119842)<[Long](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/long-0000001054439731)> persistedIds) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Checks for and deletes invalid forms of the application in the Form Manager Service based on the list of valid form IDs passed. If an empty list is passed to this method, the Form Manager Service will delete all forms of the application. **Permissions:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
persistedIds  |  Indicates the list of valid forms with persisted IDs.  
**Returns:** Returns the number of invalid forms deleted by the Form Manager Service. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
1 [h2]onForeground protected void onForeground​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Called back when the state of an ability slice changes from **BACKGROUND** to **INACTIVE**. The ability slice returns to the foreground. You can use this method to implement reinitialization or adjust the UI by using the intent parameter. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the information carried when the ability slice returns to the **INACTIVE** state.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onBackground protected void onBackground​() Called back when an ability slice enters the **BACKGROUND** state. The UI of an ability slice in the **BACKGROUND** state is invisible and not interactive. You are advised to use this method to stop or release resources. **Since:** 1 [h2]onStop protected void onStop​() Called back before an ability slice is destroyed. This method is called back after an ability slice calls the [terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method. You can use this method to reclaim resources. **Since:** 1 [h2]onAbilityResult protected void onAbilityResult​(int requestCode, int resultCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData) Called when [startAbilityForResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start an ability and the result is returned. This method provides the same function as the [Ability#onAbilityResult(int, int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method of the ability. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultCode  |  Indicates the result code returned after the ability is started. You can define the result code to identify an error.  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability is started. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [startAbilityForResult(Intent, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__startAbilityForResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) [h2]onResult protected void onResult​(int requestCode, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultIntent) Called back when the result of the [presentForResult(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) method is returned. When [presentForResult(ohos.aafwk.ability.AbilitySlice,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-) is called to start a new ability slice, the execution result is returned when the ability slice is destroyed. You can override this method to process the result. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability slice is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by ability slices. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
resultIntent  |  Indicates the data returned after the started ability slice is destroyed. This parameter can be null.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [presentForResult(AbilitySlice, Intent, int)public final void presentForResult​([AbilitySlice](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680) targetSlice, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, int requestCode) Presents another ability slice and returns the result set by the target ability slice by calling [setResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-). You can use an [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) object to pass required information. This method can be called only in the following cases:
                * The host ability of the current ability slice is in the **ACTIVE** state.
                * The target ability slice is not started or destroyed.
                * There are 1024 or less ability slices in the host ability.
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
targetSlice  |  Indicates the target ability slice. This parameter cannot be null.  
intent  |  Indicates information carried during the switching, which cannot be null.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the customized request code, which cannot be a negative number.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if targetSlice or requestCode is invalid.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [setResult(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-), [onResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]setResult public final void setResult​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) resultData) Sets the data to be returned when an ability slice is destroyed. When the ability slice calls the [terminate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__terminate--) method, the result data set by the current method is transferred to the caller. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
resultData  |  Indicates the data returned after the ability slice is destroyed. You can define the data returned. The value can be null.  
**Since:** 1 **See Also:** [presentForResult(AbilitySlice, Intent, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__presentForResult-ohos_aafwk_ability_AbilitySlice-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-int-), [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Throws this exception if the invalid forms fail to be deleted due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed persistedIds is invalid. The value of persistedIds must not be null.
                * The number of forms identified by persistedIds has reached the upper limit (256) allowed for a single application.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The forms are being restored.  
**Since:** 5 [h2]acquireFormState public void acquireFormState​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilitySlice.FormCallback](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice_formcallback-0000001071073303) callback) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Obtains the state of the form that matches the application bundle name, module name, form name, form grid style, and other related information specified in the passed intent. This method is asynchronous. After the [FormState](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formstate-0000001170214413) is obtained, [AbilitySlice.FormCallback.onAcquiredState(Intent, FormState)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice_formcallback-0000001071073303#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001071073303__onAcquiredState-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-ohos_aafwk_ability_FormState-) will be invoked to notify the application. **Permissions:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM); [SystemPermission.GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the detailed information of the form to be obtained, including the bundle name, ability name, module name, form name, and form grid style.  
callback  |  Indicates the callback to be invoked whenever the FormState is obtained.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the form state fails to be obtained due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The input parameter is null.
                * The intent does not bear the necessary bundleName, abilityName, moduleName, formName, and dimension.
                * An error occurred when sending the request to the Form Manager Service.
                * No form can be found based on the passed parameters.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) or [SystemPermission.GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED) permission.
                * The form is being restored.  
**Since:** 6 [h2]getAllFormsInfo public [List](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/list-0000001054119842)<[FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245)> getAllFormsInfo​() throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Obtains the [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) objects provided by all applications on the device. **Permission:**[SystemPermission.GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED) **Returns:** Returns a list of [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) objects if obtained; returns an empty List if no [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) is available on the device. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) fails to be obtained due to a connection error between the application and bundle manager service or because the application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED) permission.  
**Since:** 5 [h2]getAllForms [@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public [List](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/list-0000001054119842)<[FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245)> getAllForms​() throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Deprecated. Replaced by [getAllFormsInfo()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__getAllFormsInfo--). Obtains the [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) objects provided by all applications on the device. **Permission:**[SystemPermission.GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED) **Returns:** Returns a list of [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) objects if obtained; returns an empty List if no [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) is available on the device. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if [FormInfo](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/forminfo-0000001071233245) fails to be obtained due to a connection error between the application and bundle manager service or because the application is not granted with the [](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__GET_BUNDLE_INFO_PRIVILEGED), [setResult(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]onOrientationChanged protected void onOrientationChanged​([AbilityInfo.DisplayOrientation](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810) displayOrientation) Called back when the display orientation of a screen changes. You can override this method to implement your logic when the display orientation changes. By default, no operation is performed when the display orientation changes. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
displayOrientation  |  Indicates the new display orientation. The following values are supported: [DisplayOrientation#LANDSCAPE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__LANDSCAPE), [DisplayOrientation#PORTRAIT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__PORTRAIT), [DisplayOrientation#FOLLOWRECENT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__FOLLOWRECENT), and [DisplayOrientation#UNSPECIFIED](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityinfo_displayorientation-0000001054518810#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054518810__UNSPECIFIED).  
**Since:** 1 [h2]onBackPressed protected void onBackPressed​() Called back when the Back key is pressed. The default implementation destroys the ability slice. You can override this method. **Since:** 1 [h2]onKeyDown public boolean onKeyDown​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed. When any component in the AbilitySlice gains focus, the key-down event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-down event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key pressed.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-down event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyUp(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onKeyUp-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]onKeyUp public boolean onKeyUp​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is released. When any component in the AbilitySlice gains focus, the key-up event for the component will be handled first. This callback will not be invoked if the callback triggered for the key-up event of the component returns true. The default implementation of this callback does nothing and returns false. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the code of the key released.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the key-up event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if this event is handled and will not be passed further; returns false if this event is not handled and should be passed to other handlers. **Since:** 3 **See Also:** [onKeyDown(int, ohos.multimodalinput.event.KeyEvent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onKeyDown-int-ohos_multimodalinput_event_KeyEvent-) [h2]postTask public void postTask​([Runnable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/runnable-0000001054838396) task, long delayTime) Posts a scheduled Runnable task to a new non-UI thread. The task posted via this method will be executed in a new thread, which allows you to perform certain time-consuming operations. To use this method, you must also override the [Ability#supportHighPerformanceUI()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__supportHighPerformanceUI--) method. Additionally, the usage of this method must comply with the following constraints: 
                1. This method can only be used to initialize the component tree in parallel mode.
                2. The task can only be processed during system cold start.
                3. If the parallel loading mechanism is used, the component tree-related operations to be performed in [onActive()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onActive--) and [onStop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onStop--) of the ability slice must also be added to the parallel thread.
                4. You must run [setUIContent(ohos.agp.components.ComponentContainer)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setUIContent-ohos_agp_components_ComponentContainer-) to unlock the UI thread and wait until the component tree is ready.
                5. Other time-consuming operations, such as I/O and network processing, cannot be added to the parallel task queue.
**Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the Runnable task to post.  
delayTime  |  Indicates the number of milliseconds after which the task will be executed.  
**Since:** 4 [h2]onHotkeyTriggered public boolean onHotkeyTriggered​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a hotkey is pressed. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
keyCode  |  Indicates the key code.  
keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the key event is handled in this callback; returns false otherwise. **Since:** 5 [h2]onKeyPressAndHold public boolean onKeyPressAndHold​(int keyCode, [KeyEvent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/keyevent-0000001054120285) keyEvent) Called when a key is pressed and held. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description[onResult(int, Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onResult-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) [h2]terminate public final void terminate​() Destroys the current ability slice. When the current ability slice calls this method to destroy itself, the result data set by [setResult(ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__setResult-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is returned to the caller. If the slice to be destroyed is the only slice of its host ability, the ability will also be destroyed. Otherwise, the ability will not be affected. **Since:** 1 [h2]startAbility public void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) Starts a new ability. An ability slice can also use this method to start a new ability. Similar to the function of the [Ability#startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method, the system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
**Since:** 1 [h2]startAbility public void startAbility​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting) Starts a new ability with specific start settings. An ability slice can also use this method to start a new ability. Similar to the function of the [Ability#startAbility(Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__startAbility-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) method, the system locates the target ability from installed abilities based on the value of the intent parameter and then starts it. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[IllegalArgumentException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/illegalargumentexception-0000001054199724) |  Throws this exception if intent is invalid.  
**Since:** 4 [h2]startAbilityForResult public void startAbilityForResult​([Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent, int requestCode, [AbilityStartSetting](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilitystartsetting-0000001060519066) abilityStartSetting) Starts an ability with specific start settings and returns the execution result when the ability is destroyed. When the ability is destroyed, [onAbilityResult(int,int,ohos.aafwk.content.Intent)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__onAbilityResult-int-int-ohos_aafwk_content_Intent-) is called and the returned requestCode is transferred to the current method. The given requestCode is customized and cannot be a negative number. **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
intent  |  Indicates the ability to start.  
requestCode  |  Indicates the request code returned after the ability is started. You can define the request code to identify the results returned by abilities. The value ranges from 0 to 65535.  
abilityStartSetting  |  Indicates the setting ability used to start.  
**Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---

**Since:** 5 [h2]requestForm [@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public boolean requestForm​(int formId) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [requestForm(long)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__requestForm-long-). Requests for form data update. This method must be called when the application has detected that a system setting item (such as the language, resolution, or screen orientation) being listened for has changed. Upon receiving the update request, the form provider automatically updates the form data (if there is any update) through the form framework, with the update process being unperceivable by the application. **Permission:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form to update.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the update request is successfully initiated; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the update request fails to be initiated due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formId is invalid. Its value must be larger than 0.
                * The specified form has not been added by the application.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
**Since:** 5 [h2]requestForm public boolean requestForm​(long formId) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Requests for form data update. This method must be called when the application has detected that a system setting item (such as the language, resolution, or screen orientation) being listened for has changed. Upon receiving the update request, the form provider automatically updates the form data (if there is any update) through the form framework, with the update process being unperceivable by the application. **Permission:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:**

keyEvent  |  Indicates the triggered key event.

This method provides the same function as the [Ability#getOriginalDeviceId()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/ability-0000001054120007#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120007__getOriginalDeviceId--) method. **Returns:** Returns the source device ID. **Throws:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form to update.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the update request is successfully initiated; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the update request fails to be initiated due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formId is invalid. Its value must be larger than 0.
                * The specified form has not been added by the application.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.  
**Since:** 5 [h2]requestForm [@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public boolean requestForm​(long formId, [Intent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/intent-0000001054120019) intent) throws [FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 5. Replaced by [requestForm(long)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/abilityslice-0000001054678680#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678680__requestForm-long-). Requests for form data update, by passing a set of parameters (using intent) to the form provider. This method must be called when the application has detected that a system setting item (such as the language, resolution, or screen orientation) being listened for has changed. Upon receiving the update request, the form provider automatically updates the form data (if there is any update) through the form framework, with the update process being unperceivable by the application. **Permission:**[SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) **Parameters:** Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
formId  |  Indicates the ID of the form to update.  
intent  |  Indicates a set of parameters to be transparently passed to the form provider.  
**Returns:** Returns true if the update request is successfully initiated; returns false otherwise. **Throws:** Throw Name | Throw Description  
---|---  
[FormException](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/formexception-0000001071481856) |  Throws this exception if the update request fails to be initiated due to any of the following reasons: 
                * The passed formId or intent is invalid. The value of formId must be larger than 0, and intent must not be null.
                * The specified form has not been added by the application.
                * An error occurred when connecting to the Form Manager Service.
                * The application is not granted with the [SystemPermission.REQUIRE_FORM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/systempermission-0000001054238971#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054238971__REQUIRE_FORM) permission.
                * The form has been obtained by another application and cannot be updated by the current application.
                * The form is being restored.
